# Title: AI / LLM Generative-AI Blocklist (hosts)
# Description: Domains whose PRIMARY product is generative AI / LLMs (chatbots & LLM frontends, AI companions, image/video/music/voice generators,
#              AI writing/homework/humanizer tools, AI coding products, AI avatars/face-swap/deepfake, LLM APIs/playgrounds, AI tool directories).
# Homepage: https://github.com/tbpatj/blocklists
# Last modified: 2026-11-02 (America/Denver)
# Number of entries: 12119
# Exclusions: YouTube/googlevideo/ytimg, google.com, microsoft.com, bing.com, github.com, adobe.com, canva.com, social networks, news sites, stock-photo sites,
#             general cloud infra (AWS/Azure/Cloudflare roots) and "merely uses AI" products are NOT blocked. Only dedicated AI hostnames on big platforms
#             are listed (e.g. gemini.google.com, copilot.microsoft.com, firefly.adobe.com). Path-only AI features (bing.com/create, x.com/i/grok) cannot be DNS-blocked.
# Method: merged existing AI blocklists + AI-tool directories/awesome lists; keyword/category scoring; DNS-over-HTTPS liveness check (NXDOMAIN/no-A dropped);
#         NS-based parked/for-sale domain removal; manual exclusion review. See docs/sources.md.
# Run 2 (2026-11-02): +7240 AI video/image/avatar/face-swap/NSFW-gen (+voice/music) hosts from directories, CommonCrawl/NRD/top-list
#         keyword mining and similarsites lookalikes, each homepage-fetched and content-classified; DoH + NS parked checks. See docs/sources.md.
